			id = IBR_starting.10
			days = 1
		}
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = infantry_weapons

		}

			limit = { has_completed_focus = IBR_Ferrol_General }

		}

			limit = { has_completed_focus = IBR_Vimieiro_Professor }
			IBR_improve_military_opinion_salazar = yes
		}

		set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_costs_temp = 0.005 }

		}

	select_effect = {

			limit = {

			}
			IBR_legislative_council_calculate_initial_bill_position = yes
			IBR_legislative_council_initial_bill_seat_calculation = yes
			set_country_flag = IBR_show_seats_for_current_law_flag
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = { base = 700 }
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus IBR_Charter_Iberians"
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_council_active_law_temp = token:IBR_law_charter_iberians }
		IBR_council_introduce_bill = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_created_flag
				}
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_political_tie_flag }
							NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_crony_dominant_flag }
						}

							id = IBRCouncilAmendment.19
							days = 5
						}
					}
					25 = {

							factor = 0
							NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_political_tie_flag }
							NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_hardliner_dominant_flag }
						}
						country_event = {
							id = IBRCouncilAmendment.20
							days = 5
						}
					}
					25 = {

							factor = 0
							NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_political_tie_flag }
							NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_moderate_dominant_flag }

							days = 5
						}
					}

shared_focus = {
	id = IBR_approve_stabilisation_plan
	icon = GFX_focus_IBR_opus_dei_influence
	cost = 3.00
	mutually_exclusive = {
		focus = IBR_trust_the_ministries
		focus = IBR_reapply_the_old
	}
	prerequisite = {
		focus = IBR_assessing_the_economy
	}
	available = {
		AND = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_Minimum_Interaction_f }
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = IBR_deficit_management_d_tt
					hidden_trigger = {
						check_variable = { debt_to_GDP_ratio > 0.7499 }
					}
				}
				hidden_trigger = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = IBR_ECONOMIC_ROUTE option = IBR_ECONOMIC_ROUTE_OPUS_DEI }
				}
			}
			has_country_flag = IBR_economic_tree_paths
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = { base = 500 }
	x = -11
	y = 1
	relative_position_id = IBR_assessing_the_economy
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus IBR_approve_stabilisation_plan"
		hidden_effect = {
			IBR = {
				remove_ideas_with_trait = economy_minister
			}
		}
		add_ideas = IBR_Mariano_Navarro_Rubio_eco
		set_temp_variable = { econ_centralization_temp = -5 }
		econ_centralization_change = yes
		set_country_flag = IBR_chose_stabilisation_plan
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_caudillos_generic_businessmen_set_tt
		set_country_flag = IBR_caudillos_generic_businessmen_set
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
				id = IBR_economy.4
				days = 15
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = IBR_legislative_council_created_flag
				}
				add_to_variable = { Iberian_Council_reformism_var = 5 }
				custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_stabilisation_plan_tt_1
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_economy.101
					hours = 5
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_created_flag
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_economy.100
					hours = 5
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					AND = { #If the Economy is doing well, this plan backfires
						has_country_flag = IBR_Eco_Recovery_Success
						check_variable = { debt_to_GDP_ratio < 0.75 }
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_economy.102
					days = 45
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_economy.103
					days = 66
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_stabilisation_plan_tt_full_warning
			}
			#If the economy is middling or shit, this plan will work masterfully
		}
	}
}

						}

							factor = 0

							NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_moderate_dominant_flag }

shared_focus = {
	id = IBR_cutting_dead_weight
	icon = GFX_focus_IBR_dissolve_the_federal_government
	cost = 3.00
	prerequisite = {
		focus = IBR_Special_Meeting
	}
	mutually_exclusive = {
		focus = IBR_Improvements_Needed
	}
	relative_position_id = IBR_Special_Meeting
	available = {
		OR = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = IBR_advcouncil_unlock_vlow_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					check_variable = { Iberian_Council_influence_var < 20 }
				}
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = IBR_advcouncil_unlock_low_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					check_variable = {
						var = Iberian_Council_influence_var
						value = 20
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = Iberian_Council_influence_var
						value = 40
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = IBR_advcouncil_unlock_neutral_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					check_variable = {
						var = Iberian_Council_influence_var
						value = 40
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = Iberian_Council_influence_var
						value = 60
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	x = -3
	y = 1
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus IBR_cutting_dead_weight"
		country_event = {
			id = IBR_council_fate.400
		}
		country_event = {
			id = IBR_council_fate.401
			days = 1
		}
		add_ideas = IBR_Council_Dissolved
		set_country_flag = IBR_Council_Disbanded
		add_political_power = 100
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					HasIberiaInterest = yes
				}
				news_event = {
					id = WORLD.469
					days = 1
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_stability_temp = -7 }
		IBR_stability_change = yes
	}
}

shared_focus = {
	id = IBR_peace_at_last
	icon = GFX_focus_generic_democratic_unity
	cost = 3.00
	prerequisite = {
		focus = IBR_Not_Miscalculation
	}
	prerequisite = {
		focus = IBR_Dont_Get_Used
	}
	prerequisite = {
		focus = IBR_A_Special_Message
	}
	relative_position_id = IBR_Not_Miscalculation
	x = 1
	y = 2
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus IBR_peace_at_last"
		country_event = {
			id = IBR_council_fate.407
		}
		add_stability = 0.03
		add_political_power = 50
		set_country_flag = IBRDespotistUnion
		set_country_flag = IBRChoseUnion
		clr_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_created_flag
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_stability_temp = 1 }
		IBR_stability_change = yes
		set_temp_variable = { temp_law = token:IBR_iberian_council_law_adv }
		TNO_remove_assorted_law = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_OFN_focus_alignment_loss
		subtract_from_variable = { IBR_OFN_Alignment = 1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_PKT_focus_alignment_gain
		add_to_variable = { IBR_PKT_Alignment = 1 }
		hidden_effect = {
			IBR_Francisco_Franco = {
				set_portraits = {
					civilian = {
						large = "gfx/leaders/IBR/Portrait_Iberia_Francisco_Franco_70s.png"
						small = "GFX_idea_IBR_minister_Francisco_Franco_70s"
					}
				}
			}
			#Economy Balancing
			set_temp_variable = { gdp_growth_temp = -2 }
			econ_gdp_growth_change = yes
			set_temp_variable = { inflation_temp = 1 }
			econ_inflation_change = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TNO_Oil_Crisis_dynamic_modifier }
			}
			load_focus_tree = { tree = IBR_politics_despotist_union_oil keep_completed = yes }
		}
		else = {
			load_focus_tree = {
				tree = IBR_politics_despotist_union
				keep_completed = yes
			}
		}
		IBR_clear_AAS_chain_flags = yes
	}
}

shared_focus = {
	id = IBR_revive_regional_trading
	icon = GFX_focus_generic_ship_trade
	prerequisite = {
		focus = IBR_mediterranean_reconciliation
	}
	available = {
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_in_crisis
		}
	}
	cost = 2.00
	x = 25
	y = 3
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus IBR_revive_regional_trading"
		TNO_improve_trade_laws_policy_effectiveness_low = yes
		ITA = {
			set_country_flag = expanded_trade_with_IBR
			set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
			add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
		}
		TUR = {
			set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
			add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				GRE = {
					is_subject = no
				}
			}
			GRE = {
				set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
				add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				EGB = {
					country_exists = yes
				}
			}
			EGB = {
				set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
				add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				SLB = {
					country_exists = yes
				}
			}
			SLB = {
				set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
				add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ILG = {
					country_exists = yes
					is_subject = no
				}
			}
			ILG = {
				set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
				add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				SYR = {
					country_exists = yes
				}
			}
			SYR = {
				set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
				add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				SLG = {
					country_exists = yes
					is_subject = no
				}
				IBR = {
					has_government = despotism
				}
			}
			SLG = {
				set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
				add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				LEB = {
					country_exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_government = fascism }
				}
				IBR = {
					NOT = { has_government = despotism }
				}
			}
			LEB = {
				set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
				add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				LEB = {
					country_exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					has_government = fascism
				}
				IBR = {
					has_government = despotism
				}
			}
			LEB = {
				set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
				add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				EGQ = {
					country_exists = yes
				}
				IBR = {
					has_government = despotism
				}
			}
			EGQ = {
				set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
				add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ISR = {
					country_exists = yes
				}
			}
			ISR = {
				set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
				add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				PAL = {
					country_exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					has_government = communist
				}
				IBR = {
					NOT = { has_government = despotism }
				}
			}
			PAL = {
				set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
				add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				PAL = {
					country_exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_government = communist }
				}
			}
			PAL = {
				set_temp_variable = { temp_modifier = 100 }
				add_to_trade_modifier_PREV = yes
			}
		}
	}

shared_focus = {
	id = IBR_The_Iberian_System
	icon = GFX_focus_IBR_the_supremacy_of_the_caudillos
	available = {
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_in_crisis
		}
	}
	cost = 3
	prerequisite = {
		focus = IBR_The_Sum_of_Its_Parts
	}
	prerequisite = {
		focus = IBR_Season_Thaw
	}
	relative_position_id = IBR_Season_Thaw
	x = -1
	y = 1
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus IBR_The_Iberian_System"
		country_event = {
			id = IBR_auth.30
			days = 1
		}
		add_political_power = 100
		TNO_improve_admin_efficiency_med = yes
		TNO_improve_political_parties_policy_effectiveness_high = yes
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_stability_temp = 10 }
		IBR_stability_change = yes
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence_temp = 8 }
		IBR_caetano_influence_change = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence > 14 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence_temp = 15 }
			IBR_choupana_influence_change = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { # Both Below 20
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence < 20 }
					check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence < 20 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.200
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Both Above 80
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence > 79.99 }
					check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence > 79.99 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.201
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Caetano Below 20, Choupana Above 80
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence < 20 }
					check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence > 79.99 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.202
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Choupana Below 20, Caetano Above 80
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence > 79.99 }
					check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence < 20 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.203
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Both Above 20, Below 40
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence < 40 }
					check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence < 40 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.204
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Caetano Below 40, Choupana Above 40
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence < 40 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.205
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Choupana Below 40, Caetano Above 40
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence < 40 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.206
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Both Above 40, One Above 80
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence < 80 }
						check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence < 80 }
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.207
					days = 4
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

shared_focus = {
	id = IBR_Una_Grande_y_Libre
	icon = GFX_focus_IBR_una_grande_y_libre
	available = {
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_in_crisis
		}
	}
	cost = 3
	prerequisite = {
		focus = IBR_Green_Red_Yellow_Pride
	}
	prerequisite = {
		focus = IBR_The_Coming_of_Winter
	}
	relative_position_id = IBR_Green_Red_Yellow_Pride
	x = 1
	y = 1
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus IBR_Una_Grande_y_Libre"
		add_political_power = 200
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_stability_temp = 5 }
		IBR_stability_change = yes
		set_temp_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence_temp = 10 }
		IBR_caetano_influence_change = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { # Both Below 20
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence < 20 }
					check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence < 20 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.200
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Both Above 80
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence > 79.99 }
					check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence > 79.99 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.201
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Caetano Below 20, Choupana Above 80
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence < 20 }
					check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence > 79.99 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.202
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Choupana Below 20, Caetano Above 80
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence > 79.99 }
					check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence < 20 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.203
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Both Above 20, Below 40
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence < 40 }
					check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence < 40 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.204
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Caetano Below 40, Choupana Above 40
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence < 40 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.205
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Choupana Below 40, Caetano Above 40
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence < 40 }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.206
					days = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = { # Both Above 40, One Above 80
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence < 80 }
						check_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence < 80 }
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_caudillo.207
					days = 4
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
